# ex_27 -- Table 1 (fixed MLP), Supplementary Table S6 (fixed KNN), Supplementary Tables S7/S8 (tuned MLP)
# Paper: Table 1 (main text); Supplementary Table S6 (KNN, Section S6.1); Supplementary Tables S7 (tuned MLP comparison)
#        and S8 (selected configurations); search space = Supplementary Table S4
# Source: graph_hdc revision/consolidated (= revision/ex27-tables), _slurm_ex_27.py regenerated in a copy: stage fixed
#         (identical to the used _ex27/fixed_warmup.txt + fixed_gpu.txt), stage opt of the FINAL protocol (round 2:
#         every study on the full data; OPT_SUBSAMPLED = {}), stage opteval rebuilt with opteval_commands() from the
#         round-2 best configurations _ex27/jupiter/opt_configs.csv (graph_hdc 434ec3b; the Optuna journals stayed on
#         JUPITER). Archive prefixes: ex_27_warmup, ex_27_fixed, ex_27_trialfull__<method>__<target>__<n>, ex_27_opt.
# Runs: warmup_fixed 7, fixed 400, primer_opt 7, warmup_opt 161, opt 85, primer_opteval 7, warmup_opteval 8, opteval 850
#       fixed = 8 dataset groups x 5 methods x 10 seeds (one run trains MLP + KNN for all targets of its group);
#       opt = 5 methods x 17 targets (one Optuna study per line, 30 complete TPE trials each = 2550 trial runs);
#       opteval = 85 best configurations x 10 seeds (MLP only)
# Protocol: 17 Table 1 targets (corrected HOPV15 PCE/Voc indices 3/0, ClogP via mixin_clogp), columns HDF, Morgan, RDKit,
#   topological torsion, atom pair; seeds 0-9 (split + network); HDF standard encoder (2048 dims, depth 2, bidirectional,
#   total H, unit-modulus codebooks, size+diameter) with ONE codebook for all seeds (CODEBOOK_SEED 0); fingerprints 2048
#   bits, radius 2. Fixed MLP (100,100), lr 1e-3, 200 epochs, batch 64 (neural_net2, data on the GPU: NN_DEVICE_DATA);
#   KNN k=5, uniform, native metric (Jaccard for bits, cosine for HDF), exact torch search (KN_BACKEND torch). Tuning:
#   30 TPE trials per method x target on tuning seed 420 (full data), objective validation MAE; best config -> 10 seeds.
# Analysis: python analyze_ex_27.py fixed   (-> _ex27/fixed_runs.csv, fixed_summary.csv, dataset_comparison_ex27_mlp.tex
#           = Table 1, dataset_comparison_ex27_knn.tex = Table S6)
#           python analyze_ex_27.py opt     (-> _ex27/opt_runs.csv, opt_configs.csv = Table S8, dataset_comparison_ex27_opt.tex
#           = Table S7; reads the journals _ex27/optuna_full/*.log written by the opt stage). The Friedman/Holm/Wilcoxon
#           statistics of the tables come from the paper repository (scripts/table_stats.py), not from this capsule.
# Cost: paper ran on JUPITER (whole GH200 nodes, run_ex27.sbatch: warmups on CPUs, then GPU pool). fixed ~2 node jobs
#   of <4 h; a 7-target QM9 MLP+KNN run ~2.1 h on a GPU (~17 min per QM9 target on GPU, ~32 min on CPU); opt several 4-h
#   node jobs (round 2 alone: 1500 trials); opteval ~1 node job (QM9 runs at 8192 dims need up to ~8 GB GPU memory).
#   On CPU only: several thousand core-hours in total, dominated by QM9 and COMPAS-3X.
# Notes: datasets via chem_mat_data (qm9_smiles, compas_3x, hopv15_exp, aqsoldb, freesolv, bace_reg, lipophilicity).
#   The paper splits come from the JUPITER dataset caches (load__*, copied from the ex_13 checkout, other dict order than
#   a fresh download), so a re-run draws different (equally random) splits. Each opt line resumes from its journal.
#   The 35 studies outside QM9/COMPAS-3X were run in round 1 (trial prefix ex_27_trial) with identical settings and
#   copied into optuna_full; here every study runs with prefix ex_27_trialfull. The opteval lines are the configurations
#   the paper selected; if the opt stage is re-run, regenerate them with "python _slurm_ex_27.py opteval" (writes
#   _ex27/opteval_*.txt from the new journals), otherwise analyze_ex_27.py opt skips runs whose configuration is not
#   the best of the (new) study. Excluded: stages check/smoke/cpuprod (GPU equivalence check, not reported).
# @stage warmup_fixed
python predict_molecules__hdc.py --__DEBUG__="False" --__PREFIX__="'ex_27_warmup'" --SEED="0" --NUM_TEST="0.1" --NUM_TRAIN="1.0" --NUM_DATA="1.0" --DATASET_NAME="'qm9_smiles'" --DATASET_TYPE="'regression'" --NN_NUM_WORKERS="0" --NOTE="'qm9_energy'" --TARGET_INDEX="10" --EMBEDDING_SIZE="2048" --NUM_LAYERS="2" --ENCODING_MODE="'continuous'" --SPECTRUM="'unit'" --BIDIRECTIONAL="True" --HYDROGEN_COUNT="'total'" --GRAPH_ATTRIBUTES="True" --CODEBOOK_SEED="0" --DEVICE="'cpu'" --MODELS="['linear']"
